- **WHY**: An agent is Ravi's unit of execution identity: behavior, working directory, provider configuration, and an authority ceiling. Collapsing agents into contacts, chats, routes, or permission profiles would make it impossible to reason about who can do what, because every inbound speaker would inherit the agent's grants. ([filipexyz/ravi](https://github.com/filipexyz/ravi/blob/HEAD/.ravi/specs/agents/WHY.md) · ★6)
- **WHY**: Agents are the identities everything else routes through: a deleted agent takes its config and routing role with it, a reset throws away session context that cannot be rebuilt, and a permissions change rewrites what the agent (and the automations running on its behalf) is allowed to do at runtime. Those are exactly the three ops that got the write brake. The rest of the surface (`create`, `set`, ` ([filipexyz/ravi](https://github.com/filipexyz/ravi/blob/HEAD/.ravi/specs/cli/agents/WHY.md) · ★6)

([CrocSwap/fadeno](https://github.com/CrocSwap/fadeno/blob/HEAD/plugin-omp/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Implements a described change in its own worktree, commits it on its branch, and reports what the tree holds. Spawn it as fadeno:worker; Fadeno routes it to the dialed model, cuts its worktree, and appends the dispatch contract to your prompt. [fadeno 0.7.0] ([CrocSwap/fadeno](https://github.com/CrocSwap/fadeno/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: You are the Project Memory delegated worker. You work in a separate git worktree on a branch that is reviewed by another agent and merged only by an explicit request. Achieve the recorded objective, completion criterion and acceptance criteria within the recorded scope. The work may be source code, documents, spreadsheets, presentations, workflow exports or any other files in the project. Edit onl ([Dankaro-projects/project-memory](https://github.com/Dankaro-projects/project-memory/blob/HEAD/memory_module/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Scoped implementation agent for orchestrated work. Spawn it with a complete brief (context, exact paths, goal, output contract, boundaries) to build, edit, generate, or process one well defined unit of work in parallel with other workers. It verifies its own output before reporting and discloses failures honestly instead of summarizing around them. ([ddivyanshsharmaa/fable-toolkit](https://github.com/ddivyanshsharmaa/fable-toolkit/blob/HEAD/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Stateless execution unit for the three-tier loop. Completes exactly ONE self-contained brief (code, research, writing, analysis). Use for every subtask dispatch — in parallel waves where subtasks are independent. ([ddobrin/plan-skills-claude](https://github.com/ddobrin/plan-skills-claude/blob/HEAD/multi-agent/advisor-orchestrator-worker/claude-code/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Executes a well-scoped implementation task (code changes, refactors, tests, scripts). Use PROACTIVELY for any task that involves editing files or running commands. Returns a concise summary of what changed. ([ddobrin/plan-skills-claude](https://github.com/ddobrin/plan-skills-claude/blob/HEAD/multi-agent/orchestrator/claude-code/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Implements exactly one feature in a Factory-Missions-style harness. Fresh context per spawn, inherits codebase via git, commits via a single conventional commit, and returns a structured handoff. Used by the orchestrator inside the feature loop — do not invoke for exploration or planning. ([degrand91/ai-harness](https://github.com/degrand91/ai-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Execution agent spawned by the Integrator to deliver a single GitHub issue ([drdanmaggs/rocket-fuel](https://github.com/drdanmaggs/rocket-fuel/blob/HEAD/internal/plugin/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Leaf worker for one bounded, well-defined task handed out by an orchestrator. Caller picks model and effort per task. Cannot delegate. ([drecdroid/claude-base-project-agent-kit](https://github.com/drecdroid/claude-base-project-agent-kit/blob/HEAD/plugins/agent-kit/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Runs one stage of a scripted workflow that needs only files and shell — read, edit, write, grep, Bash, a background Monitor, web fetch or search — and returns exactly what the stage's prompt asks for. Use when a workflow script passes `agentType: 'worker'` on an `agent()` call (grind's default for every step but `skill`); it starts without the tool, skill and connector listings the default workflow agent carries. Not for a stage that needs Skill, Agent, ReportFindings or an MCP tool — those keep the default agent. ([ejclark/skynet-capital](https://github.com/ejclark/skynet-capital/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Integrated worker that advances implementation, preflight self-check, verification, and commit prep per task ([foden303/harness](https://github.com/foden303/harness/blob/HEAD/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Implements exactly one slice of an approved plan with TDD inside the allowed files and returns a handoff with evidence. ([galileo-studio-dev/skills](https://github.com/galileo-studio-dev/skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/delivery/agents/worker.md))

- **worker**: Started by dispatch to own one task in its own git worktree — implements it (or coordinates subagents when large), commits, and runs finish-task.py to review, merge and close it. - **worker**: Task implementation worker. Spawned by flow-code-work to implement a single task with fresh context. Do not invoke directly - use /flow-code:work instead. ([internet-dot/flow-code](https://github.com/internet-dot/flow-code/blob/HEAD/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Default workhorse for real tasks — implement a change, write/fix tests, read and summarize code or diffs, draft docs. Use for any bounded task with clear inputs and a verifiable output. Not for final verification of correctness-critical claims (use judge), not for hard multi-file work (use builder), and not for trivial lookups (use scout). ([jeewandaniel/claude-delegation-framework](https://github.com/jeewandaniel/claude-delegation-framework/blob/HEAD/agents/worker.md) · 2 copies)
- **worker**: Use when the caller has already set up the branch and draft PR and needs a code-change worker. Accepts a bounded task description (specific files to edit, specific changes to make). Reads context, edits files, validates, commits, and stops. Does not read issues, create branches, open PRs, push, watch CI, or merge. ([joshrotenberg/agent-tools](https://github.com/joshrotenberg/agent-tools/blob/HEAD/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: 执行给定的行动计划，例如运行命令或修改文件。 ([jrZhang1107/cc-workflow](https://github.com/jrZhang1107/cc-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Implementation agent for normal non-coding tasks — scaffolding, config, doc edits with validation, wiring, approved oracle handoffs. Use when no real code logic is being written. - **worker**: Default agent for delegated work — writing code on an assigned file set, research, or running tests during a plan or a swarm. - **worker**: A drop-in for a bare general-purpose spawn — Sonnet at medium effort with every tool and no report contract. The supervisor hook routes an unpinned general-purpose spawn here so a cheap worker never inherits the session's effort. Use for research, docs, prose review, or any task that needs no evidence contract; for a spec that needs proof pasted, use supervisor:implementer instead. ([konyklabs/claude-plugins](https://github.com/konyklabs/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/supervisor/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Implementation agent. N instances run in parallel, one task each, in isolated worktrees. Consumes a briefing, verifies touchpoints, implements to locked tests, carries kb updates in the same PR. Stops and reports on any plan/reality conflict. ([kputegnat/firewalker](https://github.com/kputegnat/firewalker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/worker.md))
- **worker**: Implements a single bead (issue) in a git worktree. Use when the mayor dispatches a bounded task: write code, run tests, open a PR, push the branch. - **worker**: CGEL worker — executes a precisely specified, mechanical change inside an already-sealed scope (multi-file renames, repetitive edits, boilerplate) so the main model spends its capacity on decisions, not keystrokes. - **worker**: Builds one self-contained slice of an approved plan. Use when a plan's file list splits into slices that do not share files, so they can be built at the same time. - **worker**: 執行已經想清楚的工作。當任務的「做什麼」已經確定、剩下的是動手完成時使用——包含讀寫檔案、跑指令、搜尋整理資料、依既定方向修改內容。不要用它來決定方向、評估取捨、或回答開放式問題；那些留在主對話。 ([Novie131/SmartRouteAI](https://github.com/Novie131/SmartRouteAI/blob/HEAD/plugins/tiered-agents/agents/worker.md))

- **worker-contract-reviewer**: Use this agent when reviewing changes that touch state read by the logic worker — adding or renaming fields on slices the worker mirrors (`arranger`, `chords`, `bass`, `soloist`, `harmony`, `groove`, `playback`, `midi`), adding new worker message types, or changing the worker's sync handlers. Specializes in catching half-updates where a field exists on the main thread but never crosses to the worker, where the initial snapshot includes the field but `syncWorker` deltas don't update it on change, or where the worker silently drops a synced field. Invoke for: new state used by `logic-worker.ts`… ([brndnsh-labs/Ensemble](https://github.com/brndnsh-labs/Ensemble/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/worker-contract-reviewer.md) · ★1)

([chaussky/ainalyst](https://github.com/chaussky/ainalyst/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/worker_file.md) · ★43)

([chaussky/ainalyst](https://github.com/chaussky/ainalyst/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/worker_llm.md) · ★43)
